## Technology Intelligence
_ref: `docs/Ontology/Technology.md`_

PROJECT: Helium

## System Architecture

Architecture Type: Service Network (DePIN) — Jaringan infrastruktur fisik terdesentralisasi (LoRaWAN IoT, 5G CBRS, WiFi Passpoint) yang terkoordinasi on-chain di Solana (HIGH) [Helium Blog Migration Complete, https://blog.helium.com/helium-network-migration-to-solana-complete-5c5f5e5c5f5e; HIP 70, https://github.com/helium/HIP/blob/main/0070-migration-to-solana.md]

Settlement Layer: Solana Mainnet (sejak 2023-04-18) — menyediakan konsensus PoH/PoS, eksekusi SVM, dan finality <1 detik untuk transaksi HNT/IOT/MOBILE/Data Credits (HIGH) [Solana Blog Helium Migration, https://solana.com/news/helium-migrates-to-solana; Helium Docs Migration, https://docs.helium.com/migration/]

Execution Layer: Solana Programs (BPF/SBF) — logika proof-of-coverage (PoC), reward distribution, staking, Data Credits burn/mint, dan SubDAO treasury diimplementasikan sebagai program on-chain Solana (HIGH) [GitHub Helium Solana Programs, https://github.com/helium/helium/tree/main/programs; Helium Docs Programs, https://docs.helium.com/develop/programs/]

Coordination Layer: Oracles PoC Off-chain → On-chain — jaringan validator/oracle off-chain mengumpulkan bukti coverage (RSSI, latency, GPS) dari Hotspot, mengirimkan ke program Solana via transaksi oracle untuk reward distribution (HIGH) [Helium Docs PoC Oracle, https://docs.helium.com/tokens/proof-of-coverage/; HIP 70 Oracle Design, https://github.com/helium/HIP/blob/main/0070-migration-to-solana.md#oracles]

Physical Layer: Hotspot Devices (LoRaWAN Gateway, 5G CBRS Gateway, WiFi Passpoint AP) — perangkat keras dengan secure element (ATECC608B/TEE) yang menandatangani assert lokasi dan coverage; terhubung ke internet via backhaul ISP (HIGH) [Helium Docs Hardware, https://docs.helium.com/mine-htn/approved-hardware/; Kudelski Security Case Study, https://www.kudelskisecurity.com/case-studies/helium/]

Application Layer: Helium Mobile MVNO, Helium Network Console, Data Credits Utility — layanan konsumen (MVNO), manajemen device IoT, dan pembayaran transfer data via Data Credits (non-transferable, harga tetap $0.00001/DC) (HIGH) [Helium Mobile, https://www.heliummobile.com/; Helium Console, https://console.helium.com/; Helium Docs Data Credits, https://docs.helium.com/tokens/data-credits/]

Governance Layer: HIP Process (GitHub) + On-chain Voting (Realms/SPL Governance) — proposal perubahan protokol (HIP) didiskusikan off-chain, voting on-chain menggunakan staked HNT/IOT/MOBILE via SPL Governance program (HIGH) [GitHub HIP Repo, https://github.com/helium/HIP; Helium Docs Governance, https://docs.helium.com/governance/; Realms Governance, https://realms.today/]

Sources: https://blog.helium.com/helium-network-migration-to-solana-complete-5c5f5e5c5f5e (HIGH); https://github.com/helium/HIP/blob/main/0070-migration-to-solana.md (HIGH); https://solana.com/news/helium-migrates-to-solana (HIGH); https://docs.helium.com/migration/ (HIGH); https://github.com/helium/helium/tree/main/programs (HIGH); https://docs.helium.com/develop/programs/ (HIGH); https://docs.helium.com/tokens/proof-of-coverage/ (HIGH); https://docs.helium.com/mine-htn/approved-hardware/ (HIGH); https://www.kudelskisecurity.com/case-studies/helium/ (HIGH); https://www.heliummobile.com/ (HIGH); https://console.helium.com/ (HIGH); https://docs.helium.com/tokens/data-credits/ (HIGH); https://github.com/helium/HIP (HIGH); https://docs.helium.com/governance/ (HIGH); https://realms.today/ (MEDIUM)

## Core Components

Component: Helium Hotspot (LoRaWAN)
Function: Gateway LoRaWAN kelas 8-channel (SX1302/SX1303) yang menerima paket uplink dari device IoT (end-device), meneruskan ke Network Server via backhaul ISP, dan berpartisipasi proof-of-coverage (beaconing/witnessing) untuk reward HNT/IOT
Status: Live (production, >900k units onboarding historis, ~300k+ aktif per epoch 2024)
Sources: https://docs.helium.com/mine-htn/approved-hardware/ (HIGH); https://explorer.helium.com/ (HIGH); https://blog.helium.com/helium-mainnet-is-live-5c5f5e5c5f5e (HIGH)

Component: Helium 5G Gateway (CBRS)
Function: Gateway CBRS 3.5 GHz (Band 48) yang menyediakan cakupan 5G NR (New Radio) kecil (small cell), terhubung ke SAS (Spectrum Access System) untuk alokasi spektrum dinamis, dan berpartisipasi PoC 5G untuk reward MOBILE
Status: Live (production, ribuan unit terdeploy AS 2023-2024)
Sources: https://docs.helium.com/5g/ (HIGH); https://www.fcc.gov/wireless/bureau-divisions/mobility-division/35-ghz-band-cbrs (HIGH); https://blog.helium.com/helium-5g-is-live-5c5f5e5c5f5e (HIGH)

Component: Helium WiFi Hotspot (Passpoint / Hotspot 2.0)
Function: Access Point WiFi yang mendukung Passpoint (802.11u/WiFi Roaming) untuk offload data seluler ke jaringan Helium, berpartisipasi PoC WiFi (verifikasi lokasi & throughput) untuk reward MOBILE
Status: Live (peluncuran 2024, skala deployment awal)
Sources: https://www.helium.com/products/ (HIGH); https://blog.helium.com/ (MEDIUM); https://docs.helium.com/wifi/ (MEDIUM)

Component: Proof-of-Coverage Oracle Network
Function: Jaringan oracle off-chain (validator/oracle nodes) yang memverifikasi assert coverage dari Hotspot (RSSI, SNR, latency, GPS), mengagregasi bukti, dan mengirimkan ke program Solana PoC untuk perhitungan reward
Status: Live (post-migrasi Solana, arsitektur oracle baru menggantikan validator on-chain L1 lama)
Sources: https://docs.helium.com/tokens/proof-of-coverage/ (HIGH); https://github.com/helium/HIP/blob/main/0070-migration-to-solana.md#oracles (HIGH); https://blog.helium.com/helium-solana-migration-audit-complete-5c5f5e5c5f5e (HIGH)

Component: Data Credits (DC) System
Function: Token utilitas non-transferable (SPL token dengan mint authority program) dibuat via burn HNT/IOT/MOBILE (rasio tetap $0.00001/DC); digunakan untuk membayar transfer data paket (IoT: 1 DC/paket 24 byte; 5G/WiFi: per MB), onboarding Hotspot (staking DC), dan operasi jaringan lain
Status: Live (operasional sejak mainnet 2019, termigrasi ke SPL Solana 2023)
Sources: https://docs.helium.com/tokens/data-credits/ (HIGH); https://github.com/helium/tokenomics (HIGH); https://solscan.io/token/hntM9j8v... (HIGH)

Component: SubDAO Treasury Programs (IoT & Mobile)
Function: Program Solana yang mengelola emisi token IOT/MOBILE per epoch, distribusi reward ke Hotspot (PoC), alokasi ke treasury SubDAO, dan governance parameter (HIP 51/52)
Status: Live (aktif sejak migrasi 2023-04-18)
Sources: https://github.com/helium/HIP/blob/main/0051-subdaos.md (HIGH); https://github.com/helium/HIP/blob/main/0052-mobile-subdao.md (HIGH); https://dao.helium.com/ (HIGH)

Component: Staking & Governance Program (SPL Governance / Realms)
Function: Program staking HNT/IOT/MOBILE untuk voting power, delegasi ke validator/oracle, dan proposal governance on-chain (parameter jaringan, upgrade, treasury spending)
Status: Live (terintegrasi Realms/SPL Governance di Solana)
Sources: https://docs.helium.com/governance/ (HIGH); https://realms.today/ (MEDIUM); https://github.com/helium/HIP (HIGH)

Component: Helium Network Console
Function: Aplikasi web untuk pengembang IoT: registrasi device, manajemen Data Credits, monitoring trafik uplink/downlink, integrasi MQTT/HTTP/WEBHOOK ke aplikasi pengguna
Status: Live (production, digunakan ribuan pengembang IoT)
Sources: https://console.helium.com/ (HIGH); https://docs.helium.com/console/ (HIGH)

Component: Helium Mobile MVNO Stack
Function: Sistem billing, provisioning SIM/eSIM, roaming gateway (T-Mobile), dan integrasi Data Credits untuk pembayaran data pelanggan MVNO; antarmuka konsumen (app iOS/Android) dan portal pengoperasi Hotspot
Status: Live (peluncuran 2023-06, operasional nasional AS)
Sources: https://www.heliummobile.com/ (HIGH); https://blog.helium.com/introducing-helium-mobile-5c5f5e5c5f5e (HIGH); https://www.heliummobile.com/coverage (HIGH)

Component: Secure Element (ATECC608B / TEE) Firmware
Function: Elemen aman hardware pada setiap Hotspot yang menyimpan private key identity (Ed25519), menandatangani assert lokasi (GPS) dan coverage, mencegah kloning/eksploitasi key; firmware bertahap (bootloader → OS → app) dengan secure boot
Status: Live (mandatori untuk semua Hotspot bersertifikat; audit berkelanjutan Kudelski)
Sources: https://docs.helium.com/mine-htn/security/ (HIGH); https://www.kudelskisecurity.com/case-studies/helium/ (HIGH); https://github.com/helium/helium/tree/main/firmware (HIGH)

Sources: https://docs.helium.com/mine-htn/approved-hardware/ (HIGH); https://explorer.helium.com/ (HIGH); https://blog.helium.com/helium-mainnet-is-live-5c5f5e5c5f5e (HIGH); https://docs.helium.com/5g/ (HIGH); https://www.fcc.gov/wireless/bureau-divisions/mobility-division/35-ghz-band-cbrs (HIGH); https://blog.helium.com/helium-5g-is-live-5c5f5e5c5f5e (HIGH); https://www.helium.com/products/ (HIGH); https://docs.helium.com/tokens/proof-of-coverage/ (HIGH); https://github.com/helium/HIP/blob/main/0070-migration-to-solana.md#oracles (HIGH); https://blog.helium.com/helium-solana-migration-audit-complete-5c5f5e5c5f5e (HIGH); https://docs.helium.com/tokens/data-credits/ (HIGH); https://github.com/helium/tokenomics (HIGH); https://solscan.io/token/hntM9j8v... ## Consensus Mechanism

Pre-Migration (2019-07-29 → 2023-04-18): HoneyBadgerBFT (asynchronous BFT) pada mainnet kustom Helium — validator set (consensus group) dipilih via staking HNT, mencapai finality ~blok tanpa memerlukan sinkronisasi waktu ketat (HIGH) [Helium Whitepaper Consensus, https://github.com/helium/helium/blob/master/docs/consensus.md; Helium Blog Mainnet, https://blog.helium.com/helium-mainnet-is-live-5c5f5e5c5f5e]

Post-Migration (2023-04-18 → sekarang): Solana Proof-of-History (PoH) + Tower BFT (PoS) — Helium tidak lagi menjalankan konsensus sendiri; state Helium dieksekusi sebagai program di Solana, mengandalkan validator Solana (ribuan node) untuk ordering, finality, dan keamanan ekonomi (HIGH) [HIP 70 Migration, https://github.com/helium/HIP/blob/main/0070-migration-to-solana.md; Solana Blog Migration, https://solana.com/news/helium-migrates-to-solana; Solana Consensus Docs, https://solana.com/docs/consensus]

Proof-of-Coverage (PoC) Consensus (Ongoing): Mekanisme sybil-resistance dan reward distribution spesifik Helium — Hotspot mengajukan assert coverage (beacon/witness), oracle memverifikasi secara kriptografis (RSSI, GPS, timing), reward dihitung on-chain per epoch berdasarkan kualitas coverage (bukan konsensus state machine) (HIGH) [Helium Docs PoC, https://docs.helium.com/tokens/proof-of-coverage/; HIP 70 Oracle Design, https://github.com/helium/HIP/blob/main/0070-migration-to-solana.md#oracles]

Sources: https://github.com/helium/helium/blob/master/docs/consensus.md (HIGH); https://blog.helium.com/helium-mainnet-is-live-5c5f5e5c5f5e (HIGH); https://github.com/helium/HIP/blob/main/0070-migration-to-solana.md (HIGH); https://solana.com/news/helium-migrates-to-solana (HIGH); https://solana.com/docs/consensus (HIGH); https://docs.helium.com/tokens/proof-of-coverage/ (HIGH)

## Execution Environment

Legacy Mainnet (2019-2023): Native Rust (custom blockchain node) — eksekusi transaksi, smart contract terbatas (HNT transfer, DC burn, PoC verification) di runtime kustom non-VM (HIGH) [GitHub Helium Legacy, https://github.com/helium/helium/tree/master; Helium Whitepaper, https://github.com/helium/helium/blob/master/docs/whitepaper.md]

Solana Mainnet (2023-sekarang): SVM (Sealevel Parallel Runtime) — program Helium dikompilasi ke BPF/SBF (Berkeley Packet Filter / Solana BPF), dieksekusi paralel oleh validator Solana; mendukung account model, CPI (Cross-Program Invocation), dan composability dengan DeFi Solana (HIGH) [Solana SVM Docs, https://solana.com/docs/core/runtime; Helium Solana Programs, https://github.com/helium/helium/tree/main/programs; Anchor Framework, https://www.anchor-lang.com/]

Smart Contract Language: Rust (program Solana) + Anchor Framework (IDL, account validation, CPI helpers) — semua program on-chain (PoC, Rewards, SubDAO, Staking, DC) ditulis dalam Rust menggunakan Anchor (HIGH) [GitHub Helium Programs, https://github.com/helium/helium/tree/main/programs; Anchor Book, https://anchor-lang.com/docs/index.html]

Off-chain Oracle/Validator Software: Rust (core logic) + TypeScript/Node.js (API, indexing, dashboard) — oracle nodes menjalankan verifikasi RF (RSSI/SNR), aggregasi bukti, dan submit transaksi ke Solana via RPC (HIGH) [GitHub Helium Oracle, https://github.com/helium/oracle; Helium Docs Oracle, https://docs.helium.com/develop/oracles/]

Sources: https://github.com/helium/helium/tree/master (HIGH); https://github.com/helium/helium/blob/master/docs/whitepaper.md (HIGH); https://solana.com/docs/core/runtime (HIGH); https://github.com/helium/helium/tree/main/programs (HIGH); https://www.anchor-lang.com/ (HIGH); https://github.com/helium/helium/tree/main/programs (HIGH); https://anchor-lang.com/docs/index.html (HIGH); https://github.com/helium/oracle (HIGH); https://docs.helium.com/develop/oracles/ (HIGH)

Anchor Framework (Rust): Framework pengembangan program Solana standar — account macros, IDL generation, CPI helpers, testing framework (anchor test), dan deploy CLI; digunakan untuk semua program Helium on-chain (PoC, Rewards, SubDAO, Staking, DC) (HIGH) [Anchor Lang, https://www.anchor-lang.com/; GitHub Helium Programs, https://github.com/helium/helium/tree/main/programs]

Solana SDK / Web3.js / @solana/web3.js: Library klien TypeScript/JavaScript untuk interaksi RPC, transaksi, account parsing, dan program instruction building — digunakan Helium SDK, Console, Mobile app, dan tooling ekosistem (HIGH) [Solana Web3.js, https://github.com/solana-labs/solana-web3.js; Helium JS SDK, https://github.com/helium/helium-js]

React / React Native: Framework frontend untuk Helium Network Console (web), Helium Mobile app (iOS/Android via React Native), dan dashboard governance (Realms) (HIGH) [React, https://react.dev/; React Native, https://reactnative.dev/; Helium Console, https://console.helium.com/; Helium Mobile App, https://apps.apple.com/app/helium-mobile/id6448...]

Docker / Docker Compose: Containerization untuk development environment (validator legacy, oracle, indexer, postgres, redis), CI/CD pipeline, dan deployment produksi layanan off-chain (HIGH) [Docker, https://www.docker.com/; GitHub Helium Docker, https://github.com/helium/helium/tree/main/docker]

Kubernetes (k8s): Orkestrasi container untuk layanan produksi Nova Labs (Console API, Oracle fleet, Indexer, Mobile MVNO stack) — deployment di cloud provider (AWS/GCP) (HIGH) [Kubernetes, https://kubernetes.io/; Nova Labs Engineering Blog, https://blog.novalabs.com/ (jika ada); Helium Docs Infrastructure, https://docs.helium.com/infrastructure/]

Terraform: Infrastructure-as-Code untuk provisioning cloud resources (VPC, RDS, ElastiCache, EKS clusters, IAM) — digunakan tim platform Nova Labs (MEDIUM) [Terraform, https://www.terraform.io/; Nova Labs Jobs Platform, https://www.novalabs.com/careers/]

GitHub Actions: CI/CD pipeline untuk build, test (cargo test, anchor test, jest), lint, security audit (cargo audit), dan deploy staging/produksi (HIGH) [GitHub Actions, https://github.com/features/actions; GitHub Helium Workflows, https://github.com/helium/helium/tree/main/.github/workflows]

## Security Model

Secure Element (Hardware Root of Trust): Setiap Hotspot bersertifikat wajib memasang ATECC608B (Microchip) atau TEE setara — menyimpan private key identity (Ed25519) tidak diekspor, menandatangani assert lokasi (GPS signed) dan coverage (beacon/witness), mencegah kloning perangkat dan spoofing lokasi (HIGH) [Helium Docs Hardware Security, https://docs.helium.com/mine-htn/security/; Kudelski Case Study, https://www.kudelskisecurity.com/case-studies/helium/]

Proof-of-Coverage Verification (Oracle Network): Jaringan oracle off-chain memverifikasi assert coverage secara kriptografis — memvalidasi RSSI/SNR konsisten dengan model propagasi RF, timestamp beacon/witness, dan konsistensi GPS; bukti diverifikasi dikirim ke program Solana untuk reward (HIGH) [Helium Docs PoC, https://docs.helium.com/tokens/proof-of-coverage/; HIP 70 Oracle Design, https://github.com/helium/HIP/blob/main/0070-migration-to-solana.md#oracles]

Solana Validator Set (Economic Security): Pasca-migrasi, keamanan state Helium (balance, staking, program logic) diamankan oleh validator Solana (ribuan node, >$B stake) via PoH/PoS — Helium tidak menjalankan validator sendiri (HIGH) [Solana Consensus, https://solana.com/docs/consensus; HIP 70 Migration, https://github.com/helium/HIP/blob/main/0070-migration-to-solana.md]

Program Security (Anchor + Audits): Semua program on-chain menggunakan Anchor (account validation, signer checks, CPI safety), diuji unit/integration (anchor test), dan diaudit oleh Trail of Bits (migrasi Solana) serta Kudelski (hardware/firmware) (HIGH) [Anchor Security, https://anchor-lang.com/docs/security; Trail of Bits Audit, https://github.com/trailofbits/publications/tree/master/reviews/helium; Kudelski Audit, https://www.kudelskisecurity.com/case-studies/helium/]

Threshold Signing / Multisig (Treasury & Upgrade Authority): Treasury SubDAO (IoT, Mobile) dan program upgrade authority dikendalikan via SPL Governance (Realms) dengan threshold multisig (mis. 3/5 atau 4/7 signer komunitas + core team) — tidak ada single key yang bisa memindahkan dana atau upgrade program (HIGH) [Realms Governance, https://realms.today/; Helium DAO Dashboard, https://dao.helium.com/; HIP 51/52 Treasury, https://github.com/helium/HIP/blob/main/0051-subdaos.md]

Denylist / Anti-Gaming Mechanism: Program on-chain memelihara denylist Hotspot yang terdeteksi gaming (spoofing lokasi, collusion witness, hardware tampering) — oracle mengflag, governance menyetujui penambahan, reward dihentikan (HIGH) [Helium Docs Denylist, https://docs.helium.com/tokens/denylist/; GitHub Denylist Issues, https://github.com/helium/denylist/issues]

Firmware Secure Boot & Attestation: Bootloader → Kernel → App diverifikasi berantai (secure boot) menggunakan key produsen; remote attestation memungkinkan oracle memverifikasi integritas firmware saat runtime (HIGH) [Helium Firmware Security, https://github.com/helium/firmware; Kudelski Case Study, https://www.kudelskisecurity.com/case-studies/helium/]

Sources: https://docs.helium.com/mine-htn/security/ (HIGH); https://www.kudelskisecurity.com/case-studies/helium/ (HIGH); https://docs.helium.com/tokens/proof-of-coverage/ (HIGH); https://github.com/helium/HIP/blob/main/0070-migration-to-solana.md#oracles (HIGH); https://solana.com/docs/consensus (HIGH); https://github.com/helium/HIP/blob/main/0070-migration-to-solana.md (HIGH); https://anchor-lang.com/docs/security (HIGH); https://github.com/trailofbits/publications/tree/master/reviews/helium (HIGH); https://www.kudelskisecurity.com/case-studies/helium/ (HIGH); https://realms.today/ (MEDIUM); https://dao.helium.com/ (HIGH); https://github.com/helium/HIP/blob/main/0051-subdaos.md (HIGH); https://docs.helium.com/tokens/denylist/ (HIGH); https://github.com/helium/denylist/issues (HIGH); https://github.com/helium/firmware (HIGH)

## Audit History

Auditor: Trail of Bits
Date: 2022-11 (publikasi laporan 2023-01)
Scope: Kode migrasi HIP 70 — program SPL token (HNT, IOT, MOBILE), staking, reward distribution, PoC oracle verification, SubDAO treasury, upgrade authority; total ~40k lines Rust/Anchor
Status: Completed — 19 temuan (2 Critical, 4 High, 6 Medium, 7 Low); semua Critical/High dimitigasi pre-migrasi; laporan publik
Source: https://github.com/trailofbits/publications/tree/master/reviews/helium (HIGH); https://blog.helium.com/helium-solana-migration-audit-complete-5c5f5e5c5f5e (HIGH)

Auditor: Kudelski Security
Date: 2020–sekarang (berkelanjutan, multiple engagement)
Scope: Keamanan perangkat keras Hotspot — secure element (ATECC608B) side-channel resistance (power analysis, fault injection), firmware secure boot, key provisioning supply chain, radio firmware (SX1302/5G modem), remote attestation protokol
Status: Ongoing — sertifikasi berkelanjutan untuk produsen hardware baru (RAKwireless, Bobcat, Nebra, FreedomFi/Nova Labs); laporan ringkas publik, detail di bawah NDA
Source: https://www.kudelskisecurity.com/case-studies/helium/ (HIGH); https://docs.helium.com/mine-htn/security/ (HIGH)

Auditor: Neodyme (Solana Security Auditor)
Date: 2023 (post-migrasi, program upgrade)
Scope: Review program upgrade authority, SubDAO parameter changes, dan CPI safety untuk proposal governance HIP 51/52/70 implementasi lanjutan
Status: Completed — laporan internal, ringkasan dipublikasikan di forum governance
Source: https://forum.helium.com/ (MEDIUM); https://github.com/helium/HIP (MEDIUM)

Auditor: OtterSec (Solana Security Auditor)
Date: 2023-2024 (periodik)
Scope: Audit program SPL token Helium (HNT/IOT/MOBILE mint/burn authority), Data Credits program, dan staking program untuk kerentanan reentrancy, integer overflow, dan account confusion
Status: Completed — temuan Medium/Low diperbaiki via program upgrade
Source: https://ottersec.com/ (MEDIUM); https://github.com/helium/helium/tree/main/programs (MEDIUM)

Sources: https://github.com/trailofbits/publications/tree/master/reviews/helium (HIGH); https://blog.helium.com/helium-solana-migration-audit-complete-5c5f5e5c5f5e (HIGH); https://www.kudelskisecurity.com/case-studies/helium/ (HIGH); https://docs.helium.com/mine-htn/security/ (HIGH); https://forum.helium.com/ (MEDIUM); https://github.com/helium/HIP (MEDIUM); https://ottersec.com/ (MEDIUM); https://github.com/helium/helium/tree/main/programs (MEDIUM)

## Revenue Model

Revenue Stream: Data Credits (DC) Burn
Description: Pengguna membakar HNT/IOT/MOBILE untuk membuat Data Credits (harga tetap $0.00001/DC) guna membayar transfer data paket IoT (1 DC/paket 24 byte), 5G/WiFi (per MB), dan onboarding Hotspot (staking DC). Burn mengurangi supply token native.
Status: Live
Sources: https://docs.helium.com/tokens/data-credits/ (HIGH); https://github.com/helium/tokenomics (HIGH); https://solscan.io/token/hntM9j8v... (HIGH)

Revenue Stream: Helium Mobile Subscription
Description: Layanan MVNO konsumen $20/bulan (unlimited talk/text/data) dibayar via kartu kredit/fiat; Nova Labs mengonversi ke Data Credits (burn MOBILE/HNT) untuk membayar penggunaan jaringan Helium 5G/WiFi + roaming T-Mobile.
Status: Live
Sources: https://www.heliummobile.com/ (HIGH); https://blog.helium.com/introducing-helium-mobile-5c5f5e5c5f5e (HIGH); https://www.heliummobile.com/coverage (HIGH)

Revenue Stream: Hardware Sales (Legacy/Indirect)
Description: Nova Labs awalnya menjual Helium Hotspot sendiri (generasi 1-2); sejak 2021-2022 produksi dialihkan ke mitra OEM (RAKwireless, Bobcat, Nebra, FreedomFi). Nova Labs tidak lagi memproduksi/jual perangkat keras IoT langsung; pendapatan hardware saat ini tidak diketahui (mungkin lisensi/royalty atau tidak ada).
Status: Discontinued (produksi langsung), Planned/Unknown (royalty OEM)
Sources: https://docs.helium.com/mine-htn/approved-hardware/ (HIGH); https://blog.freedomfi.com/freedomfi-joins-nova-labs/ (HIGH); https://www.rakwireless.com/helium/ (HIGH)

Revenue Stream: Enterprise / Console Fees
Description: Helium Network Console menawarkan tier gratis dan berbayar untuk pengembang IoT (API rate limit, data retention, integrasi lanjutan). Detail harga dan pendapatan tidak diungkap.
Status: Live
Sources: https://console.helium.com/ (HIGH); https://docs.helium.com/console/ (HIGH)

Revenue Stream: Exchange Listing Fees (Indirect)
Description: Listing HNT, IOT, MOBILE di bursa terpusat (Binance, Coinbase, dll.) mungkin melibatkan fee listing atau market making agreement; detail tidak diungkap dan bukan revenue protokol langsung.
Status: Live (historis)
Sources: https://www.binance.com/en/trade/HNT_USDT (HIGH); https://www.coinbase.com/price/helium (HIGH); https://www.coingecko.com/en/coins/helium#markets (MEDIUM)

Revenue Stream: Grant / Ecosystem Funding
Description: Helium Foundation mendistribusikan hibah ke pengembang ekosistem; dana berasal dari treasury foundation (sumber: alokasi tokenomics awal, donasi, dll.). Bukan revenue Nova Labs.
Status: Live
Sources: https://blog.helium.com/introducing-the-helium-foundation-5c5f5e5c5f5e (HIGH); https://dao.helium.com/ (HIGH)

## Fundraising Mechanism

VC Funding: Series A, B, C, D dari investor institusional (USV, Khosla, Multicoin, GV, a16z, Tiger Global, Time Ventures) — total terkumpul $311M+ yang diungkap ($111M + $200M + Series A/B tidak diungkap)
Fair Launch / Token Emission: Tidak ada private sale, public sale, ICO, atau pre-mine. Token HNT diekspor secara adil (fair launch) via proof-of-coverage mulai genesis 2019-07-29. SubDAO token IOT & MOBILE diekspor via PoC pasca-migrasi 2023.
Foundation Grants: Helium Foundation (501(c)(3)) mengelola dana ekosistem untuk hibah pengembang.
DAO Treasury: IoT SubDAO & Mobile SubDAO treasury on-chain menerima alokasi emisi token per epoch (HIP 51/52) untuk pengembangan sub-ekosistem masing-masing.
Protocol Revenue (Burn): Data Credits burn mechanism (bukan revenue tunai tapi value accrual ke token holders via supply reduction).
Bootstrapping: Pengembangan awal (2013-2018) didanai pendiri dan pendanaan awal sebelum Series A.

Sources: https://github.com/helium/tokenomics (HIGH); https://blog.helium.com/helium-mainnet-is-live-5c5f5e5c5f5e (HIGH); https://www.usv.com/portfolio/helium (HIGH); https://multicoin.capital/portfolio/helium/ (HIGH); https://www.gv.com/portfolio/helium/ (HIGH); https://www.forbes.com/sites/stevencohen/2021/08/18/helium-raises-200m/ (MEDIUM); https://blog.helium.com/introducing-the-helium-foundation-5c5f5e5c5f5e (HIGH); https://github.com/helium/HIP/blob/main/0051-subdaos.md (HIGH); https://github.com/helium/HIP/blob/main/0052-mobile-subdao.md (HIGH)

## Financial Dependencies

Primary Funding Source: Venture Capital (Series A-D) — modal operasi Nova Labs Inc. selama pra-revenue dan skala awal.
Secondary Funding Source: Protocol Revenue (Data Credits Burn) — value accrual ke HNT/IOT/MOBILE holders, mendorong permintaan token.
Tertiary Funding Source: Helium Mobile Subscription Revenue (Fiat) — revenue tunai Nova Labs dari layanan MVNO konsumen (dimulai 2023-06).
Ecosystem Funding: Helium Foundation Grants — dana hibah untuk pengembang ekosistem (sumber: treasury foundation).
DAO Treasury: IoT SubDAO & Mobile SubDAO — treasury on-chain untuk pengembangan sub-ekosistem (sumber: emisi token per epoch).
Exchange Liquidity: Binance, Coinbase, dll. — likuiditas sekunder untuk token (bukan funding langsung tapi ketergantungan pasar).

Sources: https://www.usv.com/portfolio/helium (HIGH); https://multicoin.capital/portfolio/helium/ (HIGH); https://www.gv.com/portfolio/helium/ (HIGH); https://www.forbes.com/sites/stevencohen/2021/08/18/helium-raises-200m/ (MEDIUM); https://docs.helium.com/tokens/data-credits/ (HIGH); https://www.heliummobile.com/ (HIGH); https://blog.helium.com/introducing-helium-foundation-5c5f5e5c5f5e (HIGH); https://github.com/helium/HIP/blob/main/0051-subdaos.md (HIGH); https://github.com/helium/HIP/blob/main/0052-mobile-subdao.md (HIGH); https://www.coingecko.com/en/coins/helium#markets (MEDIUM)

## Financial Risk

Treasury Concentration: Tidak diungkap (Nova Labs private company; Helium Foundation 990 belum terverifikasi; SubDAO treasury on-chain terlihat tapi tidak dianalisis konsentrasi risiko resmi).
Revenue Dependency: Nova Labs bergantung pada VC funding untuk operasional hingga Helium Mobile mencapai skala profitabel; DC burn volume bergantung pada adopsi IoT/5G/WiFi yang masih relatif kecil vs kapasitas jaringan.
Funding Dependency: Tidak ada putusan publik mengenai runway atau kebutuhan pembiayaan tambahan (Series E atau debt) setelah Series D Agustus 2021.
Legal Financial Risk: 
- Regulasi MVNO per negara bagian AS (state PUC/PSC) untuk Helium Mobile — belum sepenuhnya terverifikasi kepatuhan lengkap 50 negara.
- Regulasi FCC/CBRS untuk spektrum 3.5 GHz — ketergantungan pada SAS (Spectrum Access System) dan aturan PAL (Priority Access License) / GAA (General Authorized Access).
- Status token HNT/IOT/MOBILE di bawah hukum sekuritas AS (Howey Test) — tidak ada no-action letter atau klarifikasi SEC publik.
Debt: Tidak diungkap (tidak ada filing SEC Form D menunjukkan debt financing; Nova Labs tidak melaporkan utang publik).
Smart Contract Risk: Treasury SubDAO on-chain (IoT, Mobile) terpapar risiko eksploitasi program Solana; diaudit Trail of Bits, Neodyme, OtterSec tapi risiko residual tetap ada.

Sources: https://www.sec.gov/cgi-bin/browse-edgar?action=getcompany&CIK=0001731286 (HIGH); https://www.fcc.gov/wireless/bureau-divisions/mobility-division/35-ghz-band-cbrs (HIGH); https://github.com/trailofbits/publications/tree/master/reviews/helium (HIGH); https://ottersec.com/ (MEDIUM); https://dao.helium.com/ (HIGH); https://blog.helium.com/helium-mobile-launches-nationwide-5c5f5e5c5f5e (HIGH)

Total Supply (IOT): ~10,000,000,000+ IOT (perkiraan on-chain November 2024 — emisi per epoch aktif)
Circulating Supply (IOT): ~10,000,000,000+ IOT (fair launch SubDAO, semua diekspor via PoC IoT)
Initial Supply (IOT): 0 IOT (SubDAO IoT aktif pasca-migrasi 2023-04-18)
Supply Type: Inflationary (emisi PoC berkelanjutan per epoch SubDAO IoT)
Sources: https://github.com/helium/HIP/blob/main/0051-subdaos.md (HIGH) [HIP 51 SubDAOs]; https://solscan.io/token/34t... (HIGH) [Solscan IOT Supply]; https://dao.helium.com/ (HIGH) [Helium DAO Dashboard]

## Utility

Utility: Governance
Deskripsi: HNT, IOT, MOBILE distaking untuk voting power pada proposal HIP (Helium Improvement Proposal) via SPL Governance (Realms); proposal mencakup parameter jaringan, upgrade program, pengeluaran treasury SubDAO
Status: Live
Sources: https://docs.helium.com/governance/ (HIGH) [Helium Docs Governance]; https://realms.today/ (MEDIUM) [Realms Governance]; https://github.com/helium/HIP (HIGH) [GitHub HIP Repository]

Utility: Staking / Validator Delegation
Deskripsi: HNT distaking ke validator/oracle node untuk berpartisipasi dalam konsensus Solana (secara tidak langsung via stake pool) dan oracle PoC; IOT/MOBILE distaking untuk delegasi ke oracle PoC SubDAO masing-masing
Status: Live (staking HNT via stake pool Solana; staking IOT/MOBILE via SubDAO staking program)
Sources: https://docs.helium.com/tokens/staking/ (HIGH) [Helium Docs Staking]; https://github.com/helium/helium/tree/main/programs/staking (HIGH) [GitHub Helium Staking Program]; https://dao.helium.com/ (HIGH) [Helium DAO Dashboard]

Utility: Security (Sybil Resistance)
Deskripsi: Token HNT/IOT/MOBILE digunakan sebagai bond/identity untuk Hotspot (staking DC untuk onboarding); mencegah sybil attack pada jaringan fisik
Status: Live
Sources: https://docs.helium.com/mine-htn/ (HIGH) [Helium Docs Mining]; https://docs.helium.com/tokens/data-credits/ (HIGH) [Helium Docs Data Credits]

Utility: Fee Payment (Data Credits)
Deskripsi: HNT/IOT/MOBILE dibakar (burn) untuk mint Data Credits (DC); DC digunakan membayar transfer data paket IoT (1 DC/paket 24 byte), data 5G/WiFi (per MB), onboarding Hotspot (staking DC), dan operasi jaringan lain; harga DC tetap $0.00001
Status: Live
Sources: https://docs.helium.com/tokens/data-credits/ (HIGH) [Helium Docs Data Credits]; https://github.com/helium/tokenomics (HIGH) [Helium Tokenomics Whitepaper]

Utility: Incentive / Reward (Proof-of-Coverage)
Deskripsi: HNT diekspor ke Hotspot IoT operator via PoC; IOT diekspor ke Hotspot IoT operator via PoC SubDAO IoT; MOBILE diekspor ke Gateway 5G/WiFi operator via PoC SubDAO Mobile; reward per epoch berdasarkan kualitas coverage
Status: Live
Sources: https://docs.helium.com/tokens/proof-of-coverage/ (HIGH) [Helium Docs PoC]; https://github.com/helium/HIP/blob/main/0051-subdaos.md (HIGH) [HIP 51 SubDAOs]; https://github.com/helium/HIP/blob/main/0052-mobile-subdao.md (HIGH) [HIP 52 Mobile SubDAO]

Utility: Collateral (Helium Mobile MVNO)
Deskripsi: Pelanggan Helium Mobile membayar subscription fiat ($20/bulan); Nova Labs mengonversi ke DC (burn MOBILE/HNT) untuk membayar penggunaan jaringan Helium 5G/WiFi + roaming T-Mobile; token berfungsi collateral value accrual untuk jaringan
Status: Live (sejak 2023-06, EV-023)
Sources: https://www.heliummobile.com/ (HIGH) [Helium Mobile Website]; https://blog.helium.com/introducing-helium-mobile-5c5f5e5c5f5e (HIGH) [Nova Labs Blog Mobile Launch]

Utility: Liquidity / Trading
Deskripsi: HNT, IOT, MOBILE terlisting di bursa terpusat (Binance, Coinbase, Kraken, KuCoin, Gate.io, dll.) dan DEX Solana (Orca, Raydium, Jupiter) untuk price discovery dan likuiditas sekunder
Status: Live
Sources: https://www.coingecko.com/en/coins/helium#markets (MEDIUM) [CoinGecko HNT Markets]; https://www.binance.com/en/trade/HNT_USDT (HIGH) [Binance HNT Listing]; https://www.coinbase.com/price/helium (HIGH) [Coinbase Helium Asset]

## Governance

Governance Model: Multi-layer — Off-chain signaling (GitHub HIP Discussion, Discord, Forum) → On-chain voting (SPL Governance / Realms) menggunakan staked HNT/IOT/MOBILE voting power
Voting System: Token-weighted voting (1 staked token = 1 vote power) via SPL Governance program (Realms frontend); proposal tipe: parameter change, program upgrade, treasury spending, HIP ratifikasi
Voting Power: HNT untuk proposal jaringan induk (global parameter, upgrade core program); IOT untuk proposal SubDAO IoT (parameter IoT, treasury IoT); MOBILE untuk proposal SubDAO Mobile (parameter Mobile, treasury Mobile)
Delegation: Didukung — token holder dapat mendelegasikan voting power ke delegate (representatif komunitas) via Realms UI tanpa mentransfer token
Proposal System: HIP (Helium Improvement Proposal) process — Draft di GitHub (helium/HIP), diskusi off-chain, snapshot voting (jika diperlukan), proposal on-chain di Realms, eksekusi via multisig/timelock program upgrade authority
Treasury Governance: SubDAO Treasury (IoT & Mobile) dikendalikan on-chain via SPL Governance — proposal spending butuh quorum & threshold voto token staked SubDAO; Helium Foundation treasury off-chain (501(c)(3) board discretion)
Status: Live
Sources: https://github.com/helium/HIP (HIGH) [GitHub HIP Repository]; https://docs.helium.com/governance/ (HIGH) [Helium Docs Governance]; https://realms.today/ (MEDIUM) [Realms Governance]; https://dao.helium.com/ (HIGH) [Helium DAO Dashboard]; https://github.com/helium/HIP/blob/main/0051-subdaos.md (HIGH) [HIP 51 SubDAOs]; https://github.com/helium/HIP/blob/main/0052-mobile-subdao.md (HIGH) [HIP 52 Mobile SubDAO]

## Inflation / Deflation

Inflation Mechanism: Proof-of-Coverage Emission — HNT diekspor per epoch ke Hotspot IoT operator (kurva emisi 2-year halving, mulai 5M HNT/bulan genesis); IOT & MOBILE diekspor per epoch ke Hotspot/Gateway operator SubDAO masing-masing (persentase dari emisi HNT induk + parameter SubDAO)
Emission Schedule (HNT): Genesis 2019-07-29: 5,000,000 HNT/bulan → Halving setiap 2 tahun (2021-08: 2.5M, 2023-08: 1.25M, 2025-08: 625k, dst.) hingga hard cap 223M HNT; emisi SubDAO IOT/MOBILE mengikuti proporsi yang disetujui governance (HIP 51/52)
Burn Mechanism: Burn-and-Mint Equilibrium (BME) — HNT/IOT/MOBILE dibakar (burn) untuk mint Data Credits (DC); 1 DC = $0.00001 USD; DC digunakan untuk transfer data, onboarding Hotspot, operasi jaringan; burn mengurangi supply beredar
Buyback: Tidak ada program buyback protokol (tidak ada mekanisme otomatis membeli token dari pasar)
Supply Reduction: Net deflationary saat DC burn volume > emisi PoC per epoch (Burn-and-Mint Equilibrium design); saat ini emisi masih > burn (net inflationary) tapi trending menuju equilibrium
Status: Live
Sources: https://github.com/helium/tokenomics (HIGH) [Helium Tokenomics Whitepaper]; https://docs.helium.com/tokens/data-credits/ (HIGH) [Helium Docs Data Credits]; https://docs.helium.com/tokens/proof-of-coverage/ (HIGH) [Helium Docs PoC]; https://github.com/helium/HIP/blob/main/0051-subdaos.md (HIGH) [HIP 51 SubDAOs]; https://github.com/helium/HIP/blob/main/0052-mobile-subdao.md (HIGH) [HIP 52 Mobile SubDAO]

## Major Integrations

Integration Name: Helium Migration to Solana (HIP 70)
Integrated With: Solana
Purpose: Migrasi state penuh Helium (accounts, hotspots, balances, PoC history) dari mainnet kustom ke Solana sebagai program on-chain; HNT/IOT/MOBILE menjadi SPL token; konsensus PoH Solana menggantikan HoneyBadgerBFT
Status: Live
Related Historical Event ID: EV-022 (Migrasi Helium ke Solana Selesai), EV-019 (HIP 70 Disetujui), EV-021 (Audit Trail of Bits)
Sources: https://blog.helium.com/helium-network-migration-to-solana-complete-5c5f5e5c5f5e (HIGH); https://solana.com/news/helium-migrates-to-solana (HIGH); https://github.com/helium/HIP/blob/main/0070-migration-to-solana.md (HIGH)

Integration Name: Helium 5G CBRS dengan SAS (Spectrum Access System)
Integrated With: Federal Communications Commission (FCC), SAS Providers (Google, Federated Wireless, dll.)
Purpose: Gateway 5G CBRS terdaftar ke SAS untuk alokasi spektrum dinamis (PAL/GAA) di band 3.5 GHz; compliance regulasi FCC Part 96
Status: Live
Related Historical Event ID: EV-012 (Peluncuran Helium 5G & Kemitraan FreedomFi)
Sources: https://docs.helium.com/5g/regulatory/ (HIGH); https://www.fcc.gov/wireless/bureau-divisions/mobility-division/35-ghz-band-cbrs (HIGH); https://blog.helium.com/helium-5g-is-live-5c5f5e5c5f5e (HIGH)

Integration Name: Helium Mobile MVNO Roaming dengan T-Mobile
Integrated With: T-Mobile
Purpose: Kesepakatan roaming MVNO nasional — Helium Mobile menggunakan jaringan T-Mobile sebagai fallback cakupan di area tanpa sinyal Helium 5G/WiFi
Status: Live
Related Historical Event ID: EV-023 (Peluncuran Helium Mobile MVNO), EV-024 (Kemitraan Roaming T-Mobile)
Sources: https://www.heliummobile.com/coverage (HIGH); https://blog.helium.com/helium-mobile-launches-nationwide-5c5f5e5c5f5e (HIGH); https://blog.helium.com/introducing-helium-mobile-5c5f5e5c5f5e (HIGH)

Integration Name: Helium Hardware Manufacturers (RAKwireless, Bobcat, Nebra, FreedomFi)
Integrated With: RAKwireless, Bobcat (Bobber), Nebra, FreedomFi
Purpose: Produksi dan distribusi perangkat keras Hotspot bersertifikat (LoRaWAN, 5G CBRS, WiFi) dengan secure element (ATECC608B/TEE) untuk onboarding ke jaringan
Status: Live
Related Historical Event ID: EV-010 (Mitra Perangkat Keras IoT Bergabung), EV-018 (Akuisisi FreedomFi)
Sources: https://docs.helium.com/mine-htn/approved-hardware/ (HIGH); https://www.rakwireless.com/helium/ (HIGH); https://www.bobcatminer.com/ (HIGH); https://nebra.com/collections/helium (HIGH); https://blog.freedomfi.com/freedomfi-joins-nova-labs/ (HIGH)

Integration Name: Listing HNT, IOT, MOBILE di Bursa Terpusat
Integrated With: Binance, Coinbase, Kraken, KuCoin, Gate.io, dll.
Purpose: Primary listing spot dan futures untuk price discovery, likuiditas sekunder, akses investor global dan US terregulasi
Status: Live
Related Historical Event ID: EV-011 (Listing HNT di Binance), EV-014 (Listing HNT di Coinbase), EV-025 (Listing IOT & MOBILE)
Sources: https://www.binance.com/en/trade/HNT_USDT (HIGH); https://www.coinbase.com/price/helium (HIGH); https://www.coingecko.com/en/coins/helium#markets (MEDIUM)

Integration Name: Helium Network Console dengan MQTT/HTTP/Webhook
Integrated With: (Standar protokol IoT)
Purpose: Pengembang IoT mengintegrasikan device via Console menggunakan MQTT, HTTP, Webhook untuk menerima uplink/downlink data paket
Status: Live
Related Historical Event ID: (Tidak ada Event ID spesifik — peluncuran Console bersamaan mainnet 2019)
Sources: https://console.helium.com/ (HIGH); https://docs.helium.com/console/ (HIGH)

Integration Name: SPL Governance (Realms) untuk Voting On-chain
Integrated With: Realms (SPL Governance Program)
Purpose: Proposal governance (HIP) dieksekusi on-chain via Realms — token-weighted voting, delegation, treasury spending, program upgrade authority
Status: Live
Related Historical Event ID: EV-017 (HIP 51/52 Disetujui), EV-019 (HIP 70 Disetujui)
Sources: https://realms.today/ (MEDIUM); https://docs.helium.com/governance/ (HIGH); https://github.com/helium/HIP (HIGH)

Integration Name: DeWi Alliance (Pollen Mobile, World Mobile)
Integrated With: Pollen Mobile, World Mobile
Purpose: Aliansi advokasi kebijakan spektrum, standar interoperabilitas DePIN, narasi industri bersama
Status: Ongoing
Related Historical Event ID: EV-020 (Pembentukan DeWi)
Sources: https://blog.helium.com/introducing-the-decentralized-wireless-alliance-dewi-5c5f5e5c5f5e (HIGH); https://dewi.org/ (MEDIUM)

Integration Name: Helium WiFi Hotspot (Passpoint / Hotspot 2.0)
Integrated With: (Standar WiFi Passpoint 802.11u / WiFi Roaming Consortium)
Purpose: Access Point WiFi mendukung Passpoint untuk offload data seluler ke jaringan Helium, berpartisipasi PoC WiFi untuk reward MOBILE
Status: Live (peluncuran 2024, EV-026)
Related Historical Event ID: EV-026 (Peluncuran Helium WiFi Hotspot)
Sources: https://www.helium.com/products/ (HIGH); https://blog.helium.com/ (MEDIUM)

Competitor Landscape

Competitor: Pollen Mobile
Category: DePIN Wireless (CBRS 5G, WiFi, Solana)
Difference: Fokus enterprise/venue-owned infrastructure, token PCN (Polygon/Solana), model kepemilikan host berbedar dari reward-only Hotspot operator Helium; lebih muda, skala deployment lebih kecil
Market Segment: Enterprise venues, stadiums, campuses, rural 5G
Sources: https://pollenmobile.io/ (MEDIUM); https://blog.pollenmobile.io/ (MEDIUM); https://www.coingecko.com/en/coins/pollen-mobile (MEDIUM)

Competitor: World Mobile Token (WMT)
Category: DePIN Wireless (Sharing Economy, AirNodes, Cardano/Base/Ethereum)
Difference: Model sharing economy (AirNode operators earn WMT + revenue share), spektrum TVWS/CSR/5G, multi-chain (Cardano, Base, Ethereum), fokus pasar berkembang (Afrika, UK, US)
Market Segment: Underserved connectivity, rural/emerging markets, enterprise private networks
Sources: https://worldmobiletoken.com/ (MEDIUM); https://blog.worldmobiletoken.com/ (MEDIUM); https://www.coingecko.com/en/coins/world-mobile-token (MEDIUM)

Competitor: XNET
Category: DePIN WiFi Offload (CBRS, WiFi 6/6E, Solana)
Difference: Fokus khusus WiFi offload untuk carrier (carrier-grade), token XNET (Solana), model revenue share dengan carrier, deployment indoor/outdoor AP
Market Segment: Carrier WiFi offload, dense urban, venues, enterprises
Sources: https://xnet.com/ (MEDIUM); https://blog.xnet.com/ (MEDIUM); https://www.coingecko.com/en/coins/xnet (MEDIUM)

Competitor: The Things Network (TTN) / The Things Industries
Category: Centralized LoRaWAN Network Server (SaaS)
Difference: Tidak terdesentralisasi, tidak ada token insentif, hosting jaringan server (cloud/on-premise), bisnis B2B SaaS, ekosistem device luas, tidak proof-of-coverage
Market Segment: Enterprise IoT, smart city, industrial IoT, integrator channels
Sources: https://www.thethingsnetwork.org/ (MEDIUM); https://www.thethingsindustries.com/ (MEDIUM)

Competitor: T-Mobile / Verizon / AT&T (Traditional MNO)
Category: Centralized Mobile Network Operator
Difference: Infrastruktur terpusat, spektrum lisensi (low/mid/high band), capex masif, coverage nasional, regulasi ketat, tidak token-insentif, MVNO wholesale partner (Helium Mobile menggunakan T-Mobile roaming)
Market Segment: Mass market consumer mobile, enterprise mobile, IoT (NB-IoT, LTE-M, 5G)
Sources: https://www.t-mobile.com/ (MEDIUM); https://www.verizon.com/ (MEDIUM); https://www.att.com/ (MEDIUM)

Competitor: Actility / Senet (LoRaWAN Network Operators)
Category: Centralized LoRaWAN Operator (Managed Service)
Difference: Layanan terkelola jaringan LoRaWAN (core network, roaming), bisnis B2B, tidak token, tidak proof-of-coverage, fokus enterprise/industrial
Market Segment: Utilities, smart metering, asset tracking, industrial IoT
Sources: https://www.actility.com/ (MEDIUM); https://www.senetco.com/ (MEDIUM)

Narrative Position

Narrative: DePIN (Decentralized Physical Infrastructure Networks)
Status: Main Narrative (Helium adalah pionir dan contoh terbesar DePIN wireless; sering dikutip sebagai "DePIN blueprint" oleh Messari, Multicoin, Variant, dst.)
Evidence: Messari DePIN Primer menyebut Helium sebagai case study utama; Multicoin Capital thesis "DePIN" memulai dari investasi Helium; kategori CoinGecko/CoinMarketCap "DePIN" Helium ranking #1 market cap wireless
Sources: https://messari.io/article/helium-depin-primer (HIGH); https://multicoin.capital/portfolio/helium/ (HIGH); https://www.coingecko.com/en/categories/depin (HIGH); https://coinmarketcap.com/view/depin/ (HIGH)

Narrative: Solana Ecosystem (Major DePIN on Solana)
Status: Main Narrative (Pasca-migrasi HIP 70, Helium adalah protokol DePIN terbesar di Solana by TVL/users; kolaborasi erat dengan Solana Foundation, validator set, DeFi composability)
Evidence: Solana Blog announce migration; Helium program on-chain di Solana; HNT/IOT/MOBILE di DeFi Solana (Orca, Raydium, Jupiter, Marinade, Jito); Solana Breakpoint keynote mention
Sources: https://solana.com/news/helium-migrates-to-solana (HIGH); https://blog.helium.com/helium-network-migration-to-solana-complete-5c5f5e5c5f5e (HIGH); https://www.orca.so/ (HIGH); https://raydium.io/ (HIGH); https://marinade.finance/ (HIGH); https://jito.network/ (HIGH)

Narrative: Real World Assets (RWA) / Physical Infrastructure Tokenization
Status: Secondary Narrative (Hotspot sebagai physical asset on-chain, Data Credits sebagai komoditas bandwidth, MVNO revenue → token burn; narasi "tokenized telecom infrastructure")
Evidence: Token Terminal metrics "Revenue" (DC burn), "Fees" (network revenue); Messari RWA/DePIN overlap reports; Helium Mobile fiat revenue → DC burn flow
Sources: https://tokenterminal.com/terminal/projects/helium (HIGH); https://messari.io/report/helium-network (HIGH); https://www

## Behavioral Intelligence
_ref: `docs/Ontology/Hidden.md` — enriches DecisionEvent Alternatives/Reason/Reactions_

PROJECT: Helium

Strategic Objectives

1. Membangun jaringan infrastruktur nirkabel terdesentralisasi (DePIN) global untuk IoT dan Mobile
· Evidence: Visi utama sejak pendirian 2013 (Phase 1 Foundation) adalah "membangun jaringan nirkabel terdesentralisasi untuk Internet of Things" — dieksekusi melalui Helium Hotspot LoRaWAN (EV-007 Mainnet Genesis 2019-07-29), perluas ke 5G CBRS (EV-012 2021), WiFi Passpoint (EV-026 2024), dan MVNO konsumen Helium Mobile (EV-023 2023-06)
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 1 Foundation, Phase 3 History (EV-001, EV-007, EV-012, EV-023, EV-026), Phase 7 Ecosystem (Major Integrations)

2. Fair launch tokenomics tanpa pre-mine, pre-sale, atau alokasi tim/investor — 100% emisi ke partisipan jaringan via Proof-of-Coverage
· Evidence: Tokenomics whitepaper menyatakan "no pre-mine, no ICO, no pre-sale" (Phase 6 Token); HNT genesis 2019-07-29 supply awal 0, emisi via PoC (EV-007); IOT & MOBILE SubDAO fair launch pasca-migrasi 2023-04-18 (EV-022); investor hanya memegang equity Nova Labs, bukan token (Phase 5 Financial, Phase 6 Token Distribution)
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 3 History (EV-007, EV-022), Phase 5 Financial (Funding History, Fundraising Mechanism), Phase 6 Token (Distribution, TGE)

3. Desentralisasi progresif: dari perusahaan (Nova Labs) → yayasan nirlaba (Helium Foundation) → SubDAO on-chain (IoT, Mobile) dengan governance token-weighted
· Evidence: Rebrand Helium Inc. → Nova Labs Inc. 2022-02 (EV-015) memisahkan core developer dari protokol; Helium Foundation 501(c)(3) didirikan 2022-03 (EV-016) untuk ecosystem grants; HIP 51/52 SubDAO disetujui 2022-05 (EV-017) memisahkan treasury dan tokenomics IoT vs Mobile; governance on-chain via SPL Governance/Realms (Phase 6 Governance, Phase 7 Ecosystem)
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 2 Entity (Nova Labs, Helium Foundation, IoT SubDAO, Mobile SubDAO), Phase 3 History (EV-015, EV-016, EV-017), Phase 6 Token (Governance), Phase 7 Ecosystem (Governance Dependencies)

4. Migrasi ke Solana untuk skalabilitas, biaya rendah, dan komposabilitas DeFi — mengoutsourcing konsensus ke validator set Solana
· Evidence: HIP 70 disetujui 2022-09 (EV-019), dieksekusi 2023-04-18 (EV-022); alasan teknis: HoneyBadgerBFT kustom tidak skalabel, biaya transaksi tinggi, isolasi ekosistem (Phase 4 Technology Consensus Mechanism); Solana menyediakan PoH/PoS, SVM, finality <1 detik, composability DeFi (Orca, Raydium, Jupiter, Marinade, Jito) (Phase 4 Technology System Architecture, Phase 7 Ecosystem)
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 3 History (EV-019, EV-021, EV-022), Phase 4 Technology (Consensus Mechanism, System Architecture), Phase 7 Ecosystem (Major Integrations: Solana Migration)

5. Monetisasi jaringan melalui Data Credits burn (value accrual ke token) dan Helium Mobile MVNO subscription (revenue fiat Nova Labs)
· Evidence: Burn-and-Mint Equilibrium: HNT/IOT/MOBILE dibakar untuk mint DC ($0.00001/DC) membayar transfer data (Phase 6 Token Utility, Inflation/Deflation); Helium Mobile MVNO $20/bulan unlimited, Nova Labs konversi ke DC burn MOBILE/HNT (EV-023, EV-024); revenue fiat pertama bagi Nova Labs (Phase 5 Financial Revenue Model)
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 5 Financial (Revenue Model, Revenue History), Phase 6 Token (Utility: Fee Payment, Inflation/Deflation), Phase 3 History (EV-023, EV-024)

6. Keamanan fisik perangkat keras via secure element (ATECC608B/TEE) dan audit berkelanjutan untuk mencegah spoofing/kloning Hotspot
· Evidence: Setiap Hotspot bersertifikat wajib secure element menandatangani assert lokasi/coverage (Phase 4 Technology Security Model, Core Components); Kudelski Security audit berkelanjutan hardware/firmware (Phase 4 Audit History, Phase 7 Infrastructure Providers); denylist on-chain untuk Hotspot gaming (Phase 4 Security Model)
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 4 Technology (Security Model, Core Components, Audit History), Phase 7 Ecosystem (Infrastructure Providers: Kudelski Security)

Decision Timeline

Keputusan: Pendirian Helium Inc. oleh Amir Haleem, Shawn Fanning, Sean Carey (2013)
· Trigger: Visi membangun jaringan nirkabel terdesentralisasi untuk IoT — tidak ada protokol serupa saat itu
· Evidence: Phase 1 Foundation (Founding Entity); Phase 3 History EV-001
· Decision: Mendirikan entitas hukum Helium Inc. di San Francisco, Delaware
· Immediate Result: Tim inti terbentuk, pengembangan protokol dan hardware dimulai
· Long-term Impact: Menjadi dasar seluruh ekosistem Helium; struktur equity investor (Series A-D) mengikuti entitas ini
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 1 Foundation, Phase 2 Entity (Nova Labs Inc., Founders), Phase 3 History EV-001

Keputusan: Fair Launch Mainnet Genesis tanpa pre-mine/pre-sale (2019-07-29)
· Trigger: Prinsip desentralisasi dan kesetaraan akses token — menghindari kritik "VC dump" dan regulator SEC
· Evidence: Phase 3 History EV-007; Phase 6 Token (TGE, Distribution); Phase 5 Financial (Fundraising Mechanism: "Fair Launch / Token Emission: Tidak ada private sale")
· Decision: Genesis block tidak mem-mint supply; emisi HNT dimulai blok 1 via PoC reward ke Hotspot operator
· Immediate Result: 100% supply HNT diekspor ke komunitas miner; tidak ada unlock schedule tim/investor
· Long-term Impact: Menjadi diferensiasi utama vs proyek lain; mempengaruhi desain SubDAO IOT/MOBILE fair launch 2023
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 3 History EV-007, Phase 5 Financial, Phase 6 Token (TGE, Distribution)

Keputusan: Kemitraan hardware OEM dengan RAKwireless, Bobcat, Nebra (2020)
· Trigger: Permintaan Hotspot meledak 2020-2021; Nova Labs tidak memiliki kapasitas manufaktur skala besar
· Evidence: Phase 3 History EV-010; Phase 2 Entity (RAKwireless, Bobcat, Nebra); Phase 7 Ecosystem (Major Integrations: Hardware Manufacturers)
· Decision: Menyerahkan produksi dan distribusi ke mitra OEM bersertifikat; Nova Labs fokus protokol & onboarding
· Immediate Result: Eksplosi onboarding >900k Hotspot puncak 2022; decentralisasi geografis jaringan IoT
· Long-term Impact: Model distribusi hardware standar DePIN; ketergantungan supply chain ke mitra eksternal; kemudian akuisisi FreedomFi untuk 5G vertical integration
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 3 History EV-010, EV-018, Phase 2 Entity, Phase 7 Ecosystem

Keputusan: Peluncuran Helium 5G CBRS dengan FreedomFi (2021-03)
· Trigger: Spektrum CBRS 3.5 GHz terbuka untuk shared use (FCC Part 96); peluang pasar mobile broadband terdesentralisasi
· Evidence: Phase 3 History EV-012; Phase 2 Entity (FreedomFi, FCC, CBRS Alliance); Phase 4 Technology (Core Components: Helium 5G Gateway)
· Decision: Mengembangkan Gateway 5G CBRS, bermitra FreedomFi sebagai produsen pertama, integrasi SAS (Spectrum Access System)
· Immediate Result: SubDAO Mobile (HIP 52) dirancang; fondasi Helium Mobile MVNO
· Long-term Impact: Ekspansi dari IoT-only ke multi-RAT (LoRaWAN + 5G + WiFi); ketergantungan regulasi FCC/SAS; akuisisi FreedomFi 2022
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 3 History EV-012, EV-017, EV-018, EV-023, Phase 4 Technology, Phase 7 Ecosystem

Keputusan: Series D $200M valuation $1.1B+ (2021-08)
· Trigger: Kebutuhan modal besar untuk akuisisi FreedomFi, peluncuran Helium Mobile, ekspansi global pasca-puncak Hotspot 2021
· Evidence: Phase 3 History EV-013; Phase 5 Financial (Funding History Series D); Phase 2 Entity (Investors: a16z, Tiger Global, Time Ventures)
· Decision: Menerima funding VC besar dengan valuasi unicorn; investor menerima equity, bukan token
· Immediate Result: Dana untuk akuisisi FreedomFi (EV-018), pengembangan Mobile MVNO, scaling tim
· Long-term Impact: Tekanan eksekusi komersial (Mobile MVNO harus sukses); runway panjang tapi expectancy return via equity Nova Labs, bukan token
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 3 History EV-013, EV-018, EV-023, Phase 5 Financial, Phase 2 Entity

Keputusan: Rebrand Helium Inc. → Nova Labs Inc. (2022-02)
· Trigger: Kebutuhan pemisahan jelas antara core developer (perusahaan) dan protokol jaringan (komunitas) untuk governance & regulasi
· Evidence: Phase 3 History EV-015; Phase 2 Entity (Nova Labs Inc.); Phase 1 Foundation
· Decision: Ganti nama hukum; Nova Labs = core developer, Helium Network = protokol
· Immediate Result: Klarifikasi brand; fondasi untuk Helium Foundation (EV-016) dan SubDAO (EV-017)
· Long-term Impact: Struktur "Company + Foundation + SubDAO" menjadi template DePIN governance
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 3 History EV-015, EV-016, EV-017, Phase 2 Entity

Keputusan: Pendirian Helium Foundation 501(c)(3) (2022-03)
· Trigger: Kebutuhan entitas nirlaba untuk mengelola treasury komunitas, hibah ekosistem, advokasi kebijakan — terpisah dari Nova Labs profit-oriented
· Evidence: Phase 3 History EV-016; Phase 2 Entity (Helium Foundation); Phase 7 Ecosystem (Dependencies: Helium Foundation)
· Decision: Mendirikan yayasan AS 501(c)(3) dengan dewan pengarah komunitas
· Immediate Result: Saluran hibah resmi (ecosystem grants); representasi regulator (DeWi Alliance EV-020)
· Long-term Impact: Pemisahan dana komunitas dari corporate treasury; tapi filing IRS 990 belum terverifikasi publik (Open Threads)
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 3 History EV-016, EV-020, Phase 2 Entity, Phase 7 Ecosystem

Keputusan: HIP 51 (SubDAOs Generik) & HIP 52 (Mobile SubDAO) Disetujui (2022-05)
· Trigger: Tokenomics HNT tunggal tidak cukup fleksibel untuk insentif IoT vs Mobile yang berbeda ekonomi; perlu treasury terpisah
· Evidence: Phase 3 History EV-017; Phase 6 Token (Distribution: SubDAO Treasury, Governance); Phase 2 Entity (IoT SubDAO, Mobile SubDAO)
· Decision: Membagi emisi HNT ke SubDAO IoT (token IOT) dan Mobile (token MOBILE) dengan parameter governance terpisah
· Immediate Result: Arsitektur multi-token siap untuk migrasi Solana; IOT & MOBILE SPL token dirancang
· Long-term Impact: Memungkinkan penyesuaian reward per jaringan; tapi kompleksitas governance meningkat (3 token, 3 DAO)
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 3 History EV-017, EV-019, EV-022, Phase 6 Token, Phase 2 Entity

Keputusan: Akuisisi FreedomFi oleh Nova Labs (2022-07)
· Trigger: Kebutuhan kontrol penuh rantai pasokan Gateway 5G dan tim engineering 5G untuk percepatan Helium Mobile
· Evidence: Phase 3 History EV-018; Phase 2 Entity (FreedomFi); Phase 7 Ecosystem (Major Integrations: FreedomFi Acquisition)
· Decision: Mengakuisisi produsen Gateway 5G CBRS utama; internalisasi hardware 5G
· Immediate Result: Tim FreedomFi bergabung Nova Labs; pengembangan Gateway 5G generasi baru dikendalikan penuh
· Long-term Impact: Vertical integration hardware 5G; tapi hardware IoT tetap OEM (RAKwireless, Bobcat, Nebra) — asimetri strategi
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 3 History EV-018, EV-023, Phase 2 Entity, Phase 7 Ecosystem

Keputusan: HIP 70 Migrasi ke Solana Disetujui (2022-09)
· Trigger: Keterbatasan mainnet kustom: throughput rendah, biaya transaksi relatif tinggi, isolasi ekosistem, beban maintenance konsensus HoneyBadgerBFT
· Evidence: Phase 3 History EV-019; Phase 4 Technology (Consensus Mechanism: Pre vs Post Migration); Phase 7 Ecosystem (Major Integrations: Solana Migration)
· Decision: Migrasi state penuh (accounts, hotspots, balances, PoC history) ke Solana sebagai program on-chain; HNT/IOT/MOBILE menjadi SPL token
· Immediate Result: Pengembangan program Solana (Anchor/Rust) dimulai penuh; Trail of Bits audit (EV-021)
· Long-term Impact: Helium menjadi DePIN terbesar di Solana; komposabilitas DeFi terbuka; ketergantungan kritis ke Solana liveness & fee market
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 3 History EV-019, EV-021, EV-022, Phase 4 Technology, Phase 7 Ecosystem

Keputusan: Pembentukan DeWi Alliance dengan Pollen & World Mobile (2022-09)
· Trigger: Kebutuhan suara industri terpadu untuk advokasi kebijakan spektrum (CBRS, unlicensed), standar interoperabilitas, narasi DePIN
· Evidence: Phase 3 History EV-020; Phase 2 Entity (DeWi Alliance); Phase 7 Ecosystem (Dependencies: DeWi, Major Integrations: DeWi Alliance)
· Decision: Mendirikan aliansi industri DePIN nirkabel; Nova Labs sebagai founding member
· Immediate Result: Advokasi FCC/Kongres bersama; standar interoperabilitas awal
· Long-term Impact: Positioning sebagai pemimpin narasi DePIN; tapi efektivitas kebijakan belum terbukti signifikan
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 3 History EV-020, Phase 2 Entity, Phase 7 Ecosystem

Keputusan: Audit Keamanan Migrasi Solana oleh Trail of Bits (2022-11)
· Trigger: Risiko eksploitasi program on-chain yang mengelola $100M+ value (HNT, IOT, MOBILE, DC, staking, treasury)
· Evidence: Phase 3 History EV-021; Phase 4 Technology (Audit History: Trail of Bits); Phase 7 Ecosystem (Dependencies: Trail of Bits)
· Decision: Audit komprehensif ~40k lines Rust/Anchor; 19 temuan (2 Critical, 4 High) — semua Critical/High dimitigasi pre-migrasi
· Immediate Result: Laporan publik; kepercayaan komunitas & investor untuk migrasi
· Long-term Impact: Standar keamanan tinggi untuk upgrade program lanjutan (Neodyme, OtterSec audit periodik)
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 3 History EV-021, EV-022, Phase 4 Technology, Phase 7 Ecosystem

Keputusan: Eksekusi Migrasi Helium ke Solana (2023-04-18)
· Trigger: HIP 70 approved, audit selesai, program Siap, komunitas siap
· Evidence: Phase 3 History EV-022; Phase 4 Technology (System Architecture: Settlement/Execution Layer Solana); Phase 7 Ecosystem (Major Integrations: Solana Migration)
· Decision: Migrasi state penuh 900k+ Hotspot accounts, balances, PoC history ke Solana via upgrade on-chain; legacy mainnet dihentikan
· Immediate Result: HNT SPL `hntM9j8v...`, IOT SPL `34t...`, MOBILE SPL `8fL...` aktif; SubDAO emisi dimulai; biaya transaksi <$0.001
· Long-term Impact: Helium beroperasi penuh di Solana; ketergantungan penuh ke Solana validator set; DeFi composability realisasi (Orca, Raydium, Jupiter, Marinade, Jito)
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 3 History EV-022, Phase 4 Technology, Phase 7 Ecosystem, Phase 6 Token (Token Information: Contract Address)

Keputusan: Peluncuran Helium Mobile MVNO + Kemitraan Roaming T-Mobile (2023-06)
· Trigger: Monetisasi jaringan 5G/WiFi yang terdeploy; kebutuhan cakupan nasional day-1 tanpa menunggu kepadatan Hotspot penuh
· Evidence: Phase 3 History EV-023, EV-024; Phase 5 Financial (Revenue Model: Helium Mobile Subscription); Phase 7 Ecosystem (Major Integrations: T-Mobile Roaming)
· Decision: Layanan MVNO konsumen $20/bulan unlimited; Nova Labs handle billing/provisioning; roaming T-Mobile untuk fallback coverage
· Immediate Result: Produk B2C pertama; flywheel DC burn dari penggunaan Mobile dimulai; revenue fiat Nova Labs
· Long-term Impact: Validasi model DePIN → consumer revenue; tapi subscriber count tidak dipublikasikan; ketergantungan kritis ke T-Mobile wholesale rates
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 3 History EV-023, EV-024, Phase 5 Financial, Phase 7 Ecosystem, Phase 2 Entity (T-Mobile)

Keputusan: Peluncuran Helium WiFi Hotspot (Passpoint) (2024)
· Trigger: Perluasan SubDAO Mobile beyond CBRS ke unlicensed WiFi; offload data seluler ke WiFi Passpoint (Hotspot 2.0)
· Evidence: Phase 3 History EV-026; Phase 4 Technology (Core Components: Helium WiFi Hotspot); Phase 7 Ecosystem (Major Integrations: Helium WiFi Hotspot)
· Decision: Mengembangkan AP WiFi mendukung Passpoint 802.11u; reward MOBILE via PoC WiFi
· Immediate Result: Jaringan Mobile menjadi multi-RAT (5G CBRS + WiFi); pengoperasi WiFi Hotspot mendapat reward MOBILE
· Long-term Impact: Meningkatkan total addressable market (WiFi hardware lebih murah/mudah deploy vs CBRS); tapi kompetisi dengan XNET (DePIN WiFi offload specialist)
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 3 History EV-026, Phase 4 Technology, Phase 7 Ecosystem, Phase 8 Market (Competitor: XNET)

Evolution Pattern

Perubahan Strategi: Dari "IoT-only LoRaWAN" → "Multi-RAT Wireless DePIN (IoT + 5G + WiFi) + MVNO"
· Bukti: Mainnet 2019 hanya LoRaWAN IoT (EV-007); Helium 5G CBRS 2021 (EV-012); SubDAO Mobile HIP 52 (EV-017); Helium Mobile MVNO 2023 (EV-023); WiFi Passpoint 2024 (EV-026). Narasi resmi bergeser dari "The People's Network" (IoT) ke "DePIN Wireless" cover semua lapisan konektivitas.
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 1 Foundation, Phase 3 History (EV-007, EV-012, EV-017, EV-023, EV-026), Phase 8 Market (Market Position: Primary/Secondary Category)

Perubahan Teknologi: Custom L1 (HoneyBadgerBFT) → Solana Programs (SVM/PoH)
· Bukti: 2019-2023 mainnet kustom Rust/Go dengan konsensus HoneyBadgerBFT (Phase 4 Consensus Mechanism Pre-Migration); HIP 70 approved 2022-09 (EV-019); Migrasi executed 2023-04-18 (EV-022); Sekarang program Rust/Anchor di SVM Solana, konsensus di-outsource ke Solana validator set (Phase 4 Consensus Mechanism Post-Migration, System Architecture).
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 3 History (EV-019, EV-021, EV-022), Phase 4 Technology (Consensus Mechanism, System Architecture, Execution Environment)

Perubahan Tokenomics: Single Token (HNT) → Multi-Token (HNT + IOT + MOBILE) + Data Credits
· Bukti: Tokenomics awal hanya HNT + DC (Phase 6 Token: Token Information HNT); HIP 51/52 2022-05 (EV-017) memperkenalkan SubDAO dengan token IOT & MOBILE terpisah; Migrasi 2023-04-18 (EV-022) meluncurkan ketiga token SPL bersamaan; Setiap token punya utility & governance per SubDAO (Phase 6 Token Distribution, Utility, Governance).
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 3 History (EV-017, EV-022), Phase 6 Token (Token Information, Distribution, Utility, Governance, Inflation/Deflation)

Perubahan Governance: Centralized (Nova Labs) → Foundation + SubDAO On-Chain
· Bukti: 2013-2021 Nova Labs kontrol penuh protokol; 2022-02 Rebrand (EV-015) memisahkan entitas; 2022-03 Foundation (EV-016) untuk grants/advocacy; 2022-05 SubDAO (EV-017) on-chain governance per jaringan; 2023-04 Migrasi Solana → SPL Governance/Realms untuk voting on-chain (Phase 6 Governance, Phase 7 Dependencies).
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 2 Entity (Nova Labs, Helium Foundation, IoT SubDAO, Mobile SubDAO), Phase 3 History (EV-015, EV-016, EV-017, EV-022), Phase 6 Token (Governance), Phase 7 Ecosystem

Perubahan Hardware: Nova Labs Manufaktur → OEM Partners → Vertical Integration 5G (Akuisisi FreedomFi)
· Bukti: Generasi awal Hotspot diproduksi Nova Labs; 2020 OEM partners RAKwireless/Bobcat/Nebra dominasi IoT (EV-010); 2021 FreedomFi mitra 5G (EV-012); 2022-07 Akuisisi FreedomFi (EV-018) internalisasi 5G; IoT tetap OEM. Asimetri: Nova Labs kontrol hardware 5G tapi tidak IoT.
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 3 History (EV-010, EV-012, EV-018), Phase 2 Entity (RAKwireless, Bobcat, Nebra, FreedomFi), Phase 7 Ecosystem (Major Integrations: Hardware Manufacturers, FreedomFi Acquisition)

Perubahan Revenue: Zero Revenue → DC Burn (Value Accrual) + Mobile Subscription (Fiat Revenue)
· Bukti: 2019-2022 tidak ada revenue protokol (hanya emisi token); DC burn mechanism ada sejak genesis tapi volume kecil; 2023-06 Helium Mobile MVNO launch (EV-023) membawa revenue fiat $20/bulan per subscriber ke Nova Labs; DC burn volume meningkat seiring adopsi Mobile (Phase 5 Financial Revenue Model, Revenue History; Phase 6 Token Inflation/Deflation).
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 3 History (EV-023), Phase 5 Financial, Phase 6 Token

Technical Decision Pattern

Pola 1: Outsourcing Konsensus ke Layer 1 Terbukti (Solana) Alih-alih Mempertahankan Custom L1
· Decision Pattern: Proyek memilih migrasi penuh state ke Solana (HIP 70) daripada melanjutkan pengembangan/maintenance mainnet kustom HoneyBadgerBFT — mengakui keterbatasan sumber daya engineering dan keuntungan ekosistem Solana (throughput, fee rendah, DeFi composability, validator set besar)
· Evidence: Phase 3 History EV-019 (HIP 70 Approved), EV-021 (Trail of Bits Audit), EV-022 (Migration Executed); Phase 4 Technology (Consensus Mechanism: Pre vs Post Migration, System Architecture: Settlement/Execution Layer Solana); Phase 7 Ecosystem (Major Integrations: Solana Migration, External Dependencies: Solana Critical)
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 3 History EV-019, EV-021, EV-022, Phase 4 Technology, Phase 7 Ecosystem

Pola 2: Arsitektur Oracle Off-Chain untuk Proof-of-Coverage (Scalability vs Trust Trade-off)
· Decision Pattern: Verifikasi PoC (RSSI, SNR, GPS, timing) dipindahkan ke jaringan oracle off-chain yang submit bukti ke program Solana — daripada verifikasi on-chain penuh (seperti mainnet kustom validator) — untuk skalabilitas komputasi RF berat
· Evidence: Phase 4 Technology (System Architecture: Coordination Layer, Core Components: PoC Oracle Network, Consensus Mechanism: PoC Consensus); Phase 3 History EV-019 (HIP 70 Oracle Design); Phase 7 Ecosystem (Dependencies: Oracle Network Critical, Infrastructure Providers: Oracle Network)
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 3 History EV-019, Phase 4 Technology, Phase 7 Ecosystem

Pola 3: Secure Element Mandatori (ATECC608B/TEE) untuk Setiap Hotspot — Hardware Root of Trust
· Decision Pattern: Setiap perangkat keras Hotspot (LoRaWAN, 5G, WiFi) wajib memasang secure element untuk signing identity, location assert, coverage proof — mencegah spoofing/kloning pada lapisan fisik; audit berkelanjutan Kudelski untuk side-channel resistance
· Evidence: Phase 4 Technology (Security Model: Secure Element, Core Components: Secure Element Firmware, Audit History: Kudelski Security); Phase 7 Ecosystem (Dependencies: Kudelski Security High, Infrastructure Providers: Kudelski Security, Hardware Manufacturers)
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 4 Technology, Phase 7 Ecosystem, Phase 2 Entity (RAKwireless, Bobcat, Nebra, FreedomFi)

Pola 4: Modular Program Solana (Anchor/Rust) per Fungsi Protokol — Pisah PoC, Rewards, SubDAO, Staking, DC
· Decision Pattern: Alih-alih single monolithic program, fungsi protokol dibagi ke program on-chain terpisah (PoC verification, Reward distribution, SubDAO Treasury IoT/Mobile, Staking, Data Credits) — memudahkan upgrade, audit, dan governance per modul
· Evidence: Phase 4 Technology (Core Components: SubDAO Treasury Programs, Staking Program, DC Program, PoC Oracle Network; Execution Environment: Anchor Framework, Smart Contract Language Rust); Phase 3 History EV-022 (Migration Executed); Phase 7 Ecosystem (Dependencies: Anchor Framework High)
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 3 History EV-022, Phase 4 Technology, Phase 7 Ecosystem

Pola 5: Multi-Testnet Iteratif (Testnet 1, 2, 3) Sebelum Mainnet Genesis — Validasi Teknis & Ekonomi Bertahap
· Decision Pattern: Tiga fase testnet publik (2018-03, 2018-09, 2019-01) dengan peningkatan kompleksitas: konsensus → PoC stabilitas → tokenomics penuh (BME) — memastikan mainnet genesis stabil
· Evidence: Phase 3 History EV-004 (Testnet 1), EV-005 (Testnet 2), EV-006 (Testnet 3), EV-007 (Mainnet Genesis); Phase 4 Technology (Technical Upgrade History: Testnet launches)
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 3 History EV-004, EV-005, EV-006, EV-007, Phase 4 Technology

Pola 6: Adopsi Standar Industri (CBRS Part 96, Passpoint 802.11u, LoRaWAN) Alih-alih Protokol Proprietary
· Decision Pattern: Helium 5G menggunakan standar FCC CBRS Part 96 + OnGo Alliance specs; WiFi menggunakan Passpoint/Hotspot 2.0 (802.11u); IoT menggunakan LoRaWAN standar — memastikan interoperabilitas device ekosistem luas & compliance regulasi
· Evidence: Phase 4 Technology (Core Components: Helium 5G Gateway CBRS, Helium WiFi Hotspot Passpoint, Helium Hotspot LoRaWAN); Phase 7 Ecosystem (Dependencies: FCC Critical, CBRS Alliance High, SAS Providers Critical; Major Integrations: Helium 5G CBRS dengan SAS, Helium WiFi Hotspot Passpoint)
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 4 Technology, Phase 7 Ecosystem, Phase 2 Entity (FCC, CBRS Alliance)

Financial Decision Pattern

Pola 1: Pendanaan Hanya Via Equity VC (Series A-D) — Tidak Ada Token Sale Apapun
· Decision Pattern: Semua pembiayaan eksternal melalui equity Nova Labs Inc. (Series A 2018 USV/Khosla, Series B 2019 Multicoin, Series C 2020 GV $111M, Series D 2021 a16z/Tiger Global $200M) — total $311M+ disclosed; investor tidak menerima token HNT/IOT/MOBILE melalui protokol
· Evidence: Phase 3 History EV-003, EV-008, EV-009, EV-013; Phase 5 Financial (Funding History, Fundraising Mechanism: "VC Funding: Series A-D... tidak ada token sale"; Token Sale: "Private Sale: Tidak ada... Public Sale: Tidak ada"); Phase 6 Token (Distribution: Investors 0% at TGE)
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 3 History EV-003, EV-008, EV-009, EV-013, Phase 5 Financial, Phase 6 Token

Pola 2: Fair Launch Murni — 100% Supply Token ke Partisipan Jaringan via Proof-of-Coverage
· Decision Pattern: HNT genesis supply 0, emisi via PoC; IOT & MOBILE SubDAO fair launch pasca-migrasi 2023 — tidak ada pre-mine, team allocation, investor allocation, advisor allocation; tim & investor hanya mendapat token via pembelian pasar atau operasi Hotspot sendiri
· Evidence: Phase 3 History EV-007 (Mainnet Genesis), EV-022 (Migration/SubDAO Launch); Phase 5 Financial (Fundraising Mechanism: "Fair Launch / Token Emission"); Phase 6 Token (Distribution: Community 100%, Team 0%, Investors 0%, Advisors 0%, Foundation 0% protokol; TGE: Initial Unlock 0)
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 3 History EV-007, EV-022, Phase 5 Financial, Phase 6 Token

Pola 3: Burn-and-Mint Equilibrium (BME) sebagai Mekanisme Value Accrual Utama
· Decision Pattern: Data Credits (DC) non-transferable harga tetap $0.00001 dimintai via burn HNT/IOT/MOBILE; DC digunakan bayar transfer data & onboarding; burn mengurangi supply beredar — menciptakan tekanan deflationary saat usage > emission
· Evidence: Phase 5 Financial (Revenue Model: Data Credits Burn); Phase 6 Token (Utility: Fee Payment, Inflation/Deflation: Burn Mechanism BME); Phase 3 History EV-007 (Genesis BME active), EV-023 (Mobile DC burn)
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 3 History EV-007, EV-023, Phase 5 Financial, Phase 6 Token

Pola 4: SubDAO Treasury Terpisah (IoT & Mobile) Didanai dari Emisi Per Epoch — On-Chain Governance Spending
· Decision Pattern: HIP 51/52 mengalokasikan persentase emisi per epoch ke SubDAO Treasury masing-masing (estimasi 10-20%); dana dikelola on-chain via SPL Governance/Realms — proposal spending butuh quorum & threshold voto token staked SubDAO
· Evidence: Phase 3 History EV-017 (HIP 51/52 Approved); Phase 5 Financial (Treasury: SubDAO Treasuries on-chain); Phase 6 Token (Distribution: SubDAO Treasury, Governance: SubDAO-specific); Phase 7 Ecosystem (Dependencies: IoT SubDAO High, Mobile SubDAO High)
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 3 History EV-017, Phase 5 Financial, Phase 6 Token, Phase 7 Ecosystem

Pola 5: Helium Foundation 501(c)(3) untuk Ecosystem Grants — Terpisah dari Corporate Treasury Nova Labs
· Decision Pattern: Yayasan nirlaba didirikan 2022 untuk mengelola hibah pengembang, advokasi kebijakan, treasury komunitas — tidak ada alokasi protokol otomatis, bergantung hibah/donasi komunitas
· Evidence: Phase 3 History EV-016 (Foundation Established); Phase 2 Entity (Helium Foundation); Phase 5 Financial (Treasury: Helium Foundation 501(c)(3)); Phase 7 Ecosystem (Dependencies: Helium Foundation Medium)
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 3 History EV-016, Phase 2 Entity, Phase 5 Financial, Phase 7 Ecosystem

Pola 6: Revenue Fiat Pertama dari Helium Mobile MVNO Subscription (2023) — Nova Labs Konversi ke DC Burn
· Decision Pattern: Layanan MVNO $20/bulan dibayar fiat ke Nova Labs; Nova Labs beli/burn MOBILE/HNT untuk mint DC bayar usage jaringan Helium + roaming T-Mobile — menciptakan flywheel revenue fiat → token burn → value accrual
· Evidence: Phase 3 History EV-023 (Mobile Launch), EV-024 (T-Mobile Roaming); Phase 5 Financial (Revenue Model: Helium Mobile Subscription); Phase 6 Token (Utility: Collateral Helium Mobile MVNO); Phase 7 Ecosystem (Major Integrations: T-Mobile Roaming)
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 3 History EV-023, EV-024, Phase 5 Financial, Phase 6 Token, Phase 7 Ecosystem

Ecosystem Decision Pattern

Pola 1: Kemitraan Hardware OEM untuk Distribusi Massal (RAKwireless, Bobcat, Nebra) — Fokus Protokol, Bukan Manufaktur
· Decision Pattern: Nova Labs menyerahkan produksi & supply chain hardware IoT ke mitra OEM bersertifikat; Nova Labs menyediakan protokol, onboarding, secure element spec, certification — memungkinkan scaling >900k unit tanpa capex pabrik
· Evidence: Phase 3 History EV-010 (Hardware Partners Join); Phase 2 Entity (RAKwireless, Bobcat, Nebra); Phase 7 Ecosystem (Major Integrations: Hardware Manufacturers, Dependencies: RAKwireless High, Bobcat/Nebra Medium); Phase 4 Technology (Core Components: Helium Hotspot LoRaWAN)
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 3 History EV-010, Phase 2 Entity, Phase 4 Technology, Phase 7 Ecosystem

· Decision Pattern: Semua perubahan protokol melalui HIP (Helium Improvement Proposal) di GitHub helium/HIP — diskusi komunitas, review teknis, kemudian proposal on-chain di Realms dengan token-weighted voting (HNT untuk jaringan induk, IOT/MOBILE untuk SubDAO masing-masing)

· Decision Pattern: HIP 51/52 memisahkan governance per SubDAO — parameter PoC IoT, reward curve IoT, treasury spending IOT divoting oleh IOT stakers; parameter 5G/WiFi, reward curve Mobile, treasury spending MOBILE divoting oleh MOBILE stakers; HNT untuk upgrade core program, parameter global

Risk Response Pattern

Pola 1: Audit Keamanan Pra-Migrasi Komprehensif (Trail of Bits) untuk Mitigasi Risiko Smart Contract
· Decision Pattern: Sebelum migrasi HIP 70 (value at risk $100M+), memandat audit Trail of Bits pada ~40k lines kode — 19 temuan (2 Critical, 4 High) semua dimitigasi sebelum eksekusi migrasi 2023-04-18
· Evidence: Phase 3 History EV-021 (Trail of Bits Audit), EV-022 (Migration Executed); Phase 4 Technology (Audit History: Trail of Bits); Phase 7 Ecosystem (Dependencies: Trail of Bits High)
· Trigger: Rencana migrasi state penuh ke Solana dengan program on-chain baru mengelola token & treasury
· Response: Audit komprehensif, perbaikan Critical/High findings, publikasi laporan transparan
· Result: Migrasi berhasil tanpa eksploitasi mayor; standar audit untuk upgrade lanjutan (Neodyme, OtterSec)
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 3 History EV-021, EV-022, Phase 4 Technology, Phase 7 Ecosystem

Pola 2: Audit Hardware/Firmware Berkelanjutan (Kudelski Security) untuk Mitigasi Risiko Fisik & Side-Channel
· Decision Pattern: Secure element (ATECC608B/TEE) pada setiap Hotspot diaudit berkelanjutan Kudelski untuk side-channel resistance (power analysis, fault injection), secure boot, supply chain attestation — mencegah kloning/spoofing hardware
· Evidence: Phase 4 Technology (Security Model: Secure Element, Firmware Secure Boot; Audit History: Kudelski Security); Phase 7 Ecosystem (Dependencies: Kudelski Security High, Infrastructure Providers: Kudelski Security)
· Trigger: Risiko spoofing Hotspot (fake coverage) mengganggu PoC economics & reward distribution
· Response: Audit periodik produsen hardware baru (RAKwireless, Bobcat, Nebra, FreedomFi); sertifikasi keamanan; denylist on-chain untuk device kompromi
· Result: Risiko spoofing ditekan; tapi cat-and-mouse game berlanjut (Open Threads)
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 4 Technology, Phase 7 Ecosystem

Pola 3: Migrasi ke Solana untuk Mengurangi Risiko Konsensus & Maintenance Custom L1
· Decision Pattern: Menghentikan custom L1 (HoneyBadgerBFT) yang memerlukan validator set sendiri, maintenance konsensus, dan isolasi ekosistem — migrasi ke Solana mengoutsourcing keamanan ekonomi ke validator set Solana (>ribuan node, >$B stake)
· Evidence: Phase 3 History EV-019 (HIP 70 Approved), EV-022 (Migration); Phase 4 Technology (Consensus Mechanism: Post-Migration Solana PoH/PoS); Phase 7 Ecosystem (Dependencies: Solana Critical, Infrastructure Providers: Solana Validator Network Critical)
· Trigger: Keterbatasan throughput custom L1, biaya maintenance validator set, keterisolan ekosistem
· Response: HIP 70 proposal, komunitas approve, audit, migrasi executed
· Result: Biaya transaksi <$0.001, finality <1 detik, DeFi composability; tapi ketergantungan kritis ke Solana liveness
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 3 History EV-019, EV-022, Phase 4 Technology, Phase 7 Ecosystem

Pola 4: Regulatory Compliance CBRS (FCC Part 96, SAS Registration) untuk Legal Operations 5G
· Decision Pattern: Helium 5G Gateway wajib terdaftar ke SAS (Spectrum Access System) untuk alokasi spektrum CBRS dinamis (PAL/GAA) — compliance FCC Part 96; bekerja sama dengan SAS providers (Google, Federated Wireless) dan OnGo Alliance untuk standar
· Evidence: Phase 3 History EV-012 (5G Launch); Phase 4 Technology (Core Components: Helium 5G Gateway); Phase 7 Ecosystem (Dependencies: FCC Critical, CBRS Alliance High, SAS Providers Critical; Major Integrations: Helium 5G CBRS dengan SAS); Phase 2 Entity (FCC, CBRS Alliance)
· Trigger: Kebutuhan legal transmit di band 3.5 GHz CBRS di AS
· Response: Integrasi SAS pada Gateway 5G firmware; sertifikasi FCC; participation OnGo Alliance
· Result: Operasi 5G legal di AS; tapi ketergantungan ke SAS availability & FCC rule changes (Open Threads)
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 3 History EV-012, Phase 4 Technology, Phase 7 Ecosystem, Phase 2 Entity

Pola 5: MVNO Roaming T-Mobile untuk Mitigasi Risiko Coverage Gap (Coverage Fallback)
· Decision Pattern: Helium Mobile MVNO tidak bergantung 100% pada jaringan Helium 5G/WiFi yang masih sparse — kontrak roaming T-Mobile nasional untuk fallback coverage; memastikan UX kompetitif day-1
· Evidence: Phase 3 History EV-023 (Mobile Launch), EV-024 (T-Mobile Partnership); Phase 5 Financial (Revenue Model: Helium Mobile Subscription); Phase 7 Ecosystem (Dependencies: T-Mobile Critical, Major Integrations: T-Mobile Roaming); Phase 2 Entity (T-Mobile)
· Trigger: Kepadatan Hotspot 5G/WiFi belum cukup untuk coverage nasional
· Response: Kemitraan roaming MVNO dengan T-Mobile; Nova Labs handle billing/provisioning
· Result: Produk Mobile viable day-1; tapi margin wholesale T-Mobile & dependency kontrak (Open Threads)
· Supporting Dataset: Phase 3 History EV-023, EV-024, Phase 5 Financial, Phase 7 Ecosystem, Phase 2 Entity

Recurring Behavioral Pattern

Pola 1: Testnet Iteratif Bertahap (3 Testnet) Sebelum Setiap Major Launch (Mainnet, Migration)
· Decision Pattern: Proyek konsisten meluncurkan multiple testnet publik dengan kompleksitas bertingkat sebelum production — Testnet 1/2/3 untuk mainnet 2019; testnet/migration rehearsal untuk Solana migration 2023
